NIAGARA MOHAWK POWER CORPORATION
d/b/a NATIONAL GRID

of
Theodore Poe, Jr.
Dated: October 14, 2020

Corrections and Updates Testimony of Theodore Poe, Jr.
Page 1 of 6
Q.
Please state your name and business address.
1
A.
My name is Theodore Poe, Jr. My business address is 40 Sylvan Road,
2
Waltham, Massachusetts 02451.
3
4
Q.
Are you the same Theodore Poe, Jr. who previously submitted direct
5
testimony on behalf of Niagara Mohawk Power Corporation d/b/a
6
National Grid (“Niagara Mohawk” or the “Company”) in these
7
proceedings?
8
A.
Yes, I am.
The terms defined in my direct testimony have the same
9
meanings here.
10
11
Q.
What is the purpose of your corrections and updates testimony?
12
A.
The purpose of my corrections and updates testimony is to update the
13
Company’s forecast of projected gas sales for the Rate Year (July 1, 2021
14
to June 30, 2022) and Data Years (July 1, 2022 to June 30, 2023 and July 1,
15
2023 to June 30, 2024) based on actual data through July 2020.
In
16
particular, the Company:
17
18

Updated the normal heating degree days (“HDD”) and normal billing
19
degree days (“BDD”) for the forecast period to the 30-year average
20
(1990 – 2019);
21


--- Page 3 ---
Corrections and Updates Testimony of Theodore Poe, Jr.
Page 2 of 6

Updated the use-per-customer model forecasts with the new normal
1
BDD;
2

Updated the historical meter count and volume (and use-per-customer)
3
data through July 2020;
4

Updated the normalization of the historical data to reflect the updated
5
normal HDD and BDD;
6

Removed the adjustment made in the initial filing forecast for the
7
months of March through June 2020 to reflect the anticipated impact of
8
the COVID-19 pandemic because that data is now reflected in the actual
9
historical data; and
10

Corrected the forecast implementation of the proposed service
11
classification adjustments described on pages 13 and 14 of my direct
12
testimony to reflect the impact of this proposal properly.
13
14
Q.
Does your corrections and updates testimony include any exhibits?
15
A.
Yes. My corrections and updates testimony includes the following exhibits
16
that were prepared or compiled under my direction and supervision:

Corrections and Updates Testimony of Theodore Poe, Jr.
Page 4 of 6
Q.
Please describe the updates to Niagara Mohawk’s gas sales forecast.
1
A.
My direct testimony and exhibits provided forecasts of the Company’s gas
2
sales for the Rate Year and Data Years using data through February 2020.
3
The Company recently received actual data through July 2020 and used that
4
data to update the gas sales forecasts for the Rate Year and Data Years.
5
Historical data, as presented in the updated exhibits, is now available
6
through July 2020. The forecast data presented in the updated exhibits now
7
begins in August 2020.
8
9
Incorporating actual data through July 2020 plus the other modifications
10
results in an increase in the Rate Year gas sales forecast from 1,656 million
11
therms to 1,685 million therms and an increase from 631,049 customers to
12
632,113 customers (as measured in January of the Rate Year). Although
13
there was no change made in the economic forecast between the two gas
14
sales forecasts, the inclusion of the additional months of customer data is
15
one principal driver of these changes. The impacts of the increase in the
16
Company’s gas sales forecast has been reflected in the corrections and
17
updates testimonies of the Gas Rate Design Panel and the Revenue
18
Requirements Panel.
19
20


--- Page 6 ---
Corrections and Updates Testimony of Theodore Poe, Jr.
Page 5 of 6
Q.
Please explain how the impact of the COVID-19 pandemic has been
1
reflected in the gas sales forecast.
2
A.
The forecast filed in my direct testimony reflected the anticipated economic
3
impact of the COVID-19 pandemic by incorporating Moody’s April 2020
4
economic forecast, which accounted for the pandemic. Additionally, the
5
Company adjusted the volumes for the period March – June 2020 to reflect
6
the impacts on customer sales it was observing since the beginning of the
7
pandemic. In its corrections and updates forecast, the Company continues
8
to use Moody’s April 2020 economic forecast.
While it has been
9
monitoring updates to the Moody’s forecasts on a monthly basis, the April
10
2020 forecast remains a proper reflection of the expected impact of the
11
pandemic on the economy in Niagara Mohawk’s service territory.
12
However, because the Company now has historical data (including
13
customer sales) through July 2020, the March – June 2020 adjustment is no
14
longer needed, as the historical data reflects the impact of the pandemic.
15
16
Q.
Please explain the correction to the gas sales forecast to reflect the
17
impact of certain service classification adjustments.
18
A.
As explained at pages 13 and 14 of my direct testimony, the initial gas sales
19
forecast was adjusted to reflect the proposals made by the Gas Rate Design
20
Panel to reassign existing SC3 customers to other service classifications, to
21


--- Page 7 ---
Corrections and Updates Testimony of Theodore Poe, Jr.
Page 6 of 6
require all new SC5, SC7, or SC8 daily-balanced transportation customers
1
to join the system as capacity-eligible customers, and to reassign certain
2
SC5 and SC7 customers to service classes more appropriate to their usage.
3
In making the adjustment, however, I had double-counted some meters
4
moving into East Gate SC7 Sales, which led to 30 excess meters in the SC7
5
meter count. The updated sales forecast corrects this error and the SC7
6
meter count is now accurate. The impact on the overall gas sales forecast
7
was negligible.
8
9
Q.
Does this conclude your corrections and updates testimony?
10
A.
Yes.
11
